([meisijiya/EnjoyCode_omo](https://github.com/meisijiya/EnjoyCode_omo/tree/HEAD/.opencode/skills/pm-intended-vs-implemented))
- **pm-interaction-design**: Design the user-facing interaction for AI products (AI 产品交互设计/人机交互). Use when deciding how to show waiting and streaming output (等待/流式输出/进度感), whether and how to expose uncertainty or confidence (不确定性/置信度), how much reasoning and which citations to surface (可解释/溯源/引用展示), how users tell the product it got something wrong and how it recovers (纠错/反馈), undo and safety confirmations for risky actions (可撤销/二次确认), the UI for handing off to and back from a human (人机接管), context cues in multi-turn conversations (多轮对话/上下文提示), empty states and onboarding when users don't know what to ask (空状态/引导), inter… ([chencccccccc/ai-pm](https://github.com/chencccccccc/ai-pm/tree/HEAD/skills/pm-interaction-design))

- **pm-interview**: The grilling engine — one decision-bearing question at a time, always led by a recommended answer and why. Consult when a pm-os skill needs the user to decide, scope, confirm, or correct anything. Rules: 2–3 questions only in breadth phases; look facts up yourself so only decisions reach the human; assert and invite correction ("I'd suggest X because Y — correct me"); state the round budget up front; never act until shared understanding.
